select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='2fa16cfd-a534-4bce-8111-ea033207f5c6-1764793957' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '2fa16cfd-a534-4bce-8111-ea033207f5c6-1764793957' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1184 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='50a13bf1-f1de-4a10-9c57-0c0dd79bd959-1765745516' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='50a13bf1-f1de-4a10-9c57-0c0dd79bd959-1765745516' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '50a13bf1-f1de-4a10-9c57-0c0dd79bd959-1765745516' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1388 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='ef3d81e0-a333-4c13-b933-6bae74e0225f-1767889558' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='ef3d81e0-a333-4c13-b933-6bae74e0225f-1767889558' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'ef3d81e0-a333-4c13-b933-6bae74e0225f-1767889558' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='a51e8628-6e79-4f5a-bc4a-0e5933b31635-1767890199' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='a51e8628-6e79-4f5a-bc4a-0e5933b31635-1767890199' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

select is_vip, is_free_time, guid, id, rfid, name, dob, email, phone, balance_main, balance_bonus, card_sno, created_at, total_token from es_customer where is_new=1 and rfid='{398E906F}'
select ifnull(max(created_at_unix), 0) as uts from es_billing where rfid='{398E906F}'
select * from es_billing where rfid='{398E906F}' and guid='15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669'
select * from es_softplay_customer_slots where rfid='{398E906F}' and customer_id='13'
select * from es_sale where rfid='{398E906F}'
 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398E906F}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=13 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{398E906F}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=13 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '15becfbd-3078-4b8e-98b0-8b2ec828339c-1759838669' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select ei.id, CONCAT('Token: ' , ei.token_count, ', Balance Qty: ', (ei.qty - get_used_item_qty(ei.id)) , ' , Item: ',  ei.name) AS n from es_item as ei WHERE ei.qty > 0 and ei.token_count > 0  order by ei.token_count, ei.name
select is_vip, is_free_time, guid, id, rfid, name, dob, email, phone, balance_main, balance_bonus, card_sno, created_at, total_token from es_customer where is_new=1 and rfid='{650D7B5A}'
select ifnull(max(created_at_unix), 0) as uts from es_billing where rfid='{650D7B5A}'
select * from es_billing where rfid='{650D7B5A}' and guid='ee9a4072-5be4-4b56-ab16-53feca355fe9-1767636178'
select * from es_softplay_customer_slots where rfid='{650D7B5A}' and customer_id='1365'
select * from es_sale where rfid='{650D7B5A}'
 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'ee9a4072-5be4-4b56-ab16-53feca355fe9-1767636178' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{650D7B5A}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1365 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='ee9a4072-5be4-4b56-ab16-53feca355fe9-1767636178' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='ee9a4072-5be4-4b56-ab16-53feca355fe9-1767636178' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'ee9a4072-5be4-4b56-ab16-53feca355fe9-1767636178' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select * from es_softplay_customer_slots where rfid='{39106100}' and customer_id='1231'

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'b9553162-3d8c-4763-8f89-b7254c36c9e8-1766676054' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{39106100}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1231 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='b9553162-3d8c-4763-8f89-b7254c36c9e8-1766676054' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='b9553162-3d8c-4763-8f89-b7254c36c9e8-1766676054' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'b9553162-3d8c-4763-8f89-b7254c36c9e8-1766676054' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select count(1) as return_count from es_payment where payment_mode = 'RETURN CARD CASH' and created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59'
select payment_mode, sum(recharge_amount - discount_amount_on_bill) as ra from es_payment where created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59' GROUP BY payment_mode order BY payment_mode
select IFNULL(sum(amt),0) as sa from es_payment_adjustment where dept='GAME' and dt >='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and dt<='2026-01-08 23:59:59'
select payment_mode, sum(total_amount) as ra from es_customer_direct_sale where created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59' GROUP BY payment_mode order BY payment_mode
select IFNULL(sum(amt),0) as sa from es_payment_adjustment where dept='DIRECT SALE' and dt >='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and dt<='2026-01-08 23:59:59'
select log_game, count(1) as qa, sum(amount) as ga from es_billing where created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59' group by log_game order BY log_game
select item_id, item_name, sum(item_qty) as qa, sum(final_gross_amount) as da from  es_customer_direct_sale_detail where created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59'  group by item_id, item_name order by item_name, item_id
SELECT recharge_amount , COUNT(recharge_amount) as c from es_payment WHERE recharge_amount IN (SELECT amount FROM es_bonus) and created_at>='2026-01-08 00:00:00' and created_at<='2026-01-08 23:59:59'  GROUP BY recharge_amount ORDER BY recharge_amount;
